- [Volume Offers — Volume Lifecycle / Shared Disk Capacity](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/volume-offers#volume-lifecycle-shared-disk-capacity) — `VOL-C13` — “2. **Publish a volume offer.** Create it with the machine listing or list storage separately. This advertises a maximum rentable capacity; it does not create a renter volume. The capacity in a volume offer is the maximum amount renters may allocate; publishing the offer does not itself create a renter volume. When a renter creates a volume, its requested size is allocated from that offer and must be included when you plan the shared disk pool.” — **Responsible role:** Volumes allocation backend source owner; Authorized Volumes Host/renter operator. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Bind the allocation implementation and retain offer-only, partial, exact, and over-capacity observations.

The metapackage depends on both the image and the matching headers, which is why `linux-headers-5.15.0-190-generic` appears above. DKMS needs headers matching the kernel it is building for, and the NVIDIA driver is built by DKMS. Install an image on its own and the driver has nothing to rebuild against, so the GPUs disappear on the next boot.” — **Responsible role:** Host implementation source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Bind an exact canonical source, authorized runtime observation, or accountable-owner decision before changing status.

- [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — Check basic connectivity](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#check-basic-connectivity) — `CHC-MACHINE-OFFLINE-ICMP-SCOPE-01` — “This command sends 4 test packets directly to Cloudflare's public IP address (`1.1.1.1`). It tests reachability of that IP over ICMP while bypassing DNS. A successful result does not prove that DNS or every outbound service works; if the next check fails, treat name resolution and target reachability as separate signals to investigate.” — **Responsible role:** Network or API source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Obtain network-owner confirmation before publishing a stronger diagnosis.
- [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — Check basic connectivity](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#check-basic-connectivity) — `CHC-MACHINE-OFFLINE-ICMP-OUTAGE-01` — “What to look for: **`4 packets transmitted, 4 received, 0% packet loss`** shows that this ICMP test received replies. `100% packet loss`, `Network is unreachable`, or a hang is not conclusive proof of a general outbound outage: ICMP can be filtered, rate-limited, or blocked on one path. Compare it with the DNS and web-request checks below, then involve the network owner before changing cables, routing, or firewall configuration.” — **Responsible role:** Network or API source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Obtain network-owner confirmation before publishing a stronger diagnosis or a configuration change.
- [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — Check DNS resolution](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#check-dns-resolution) — `CUR-6f6c9db3f66f6420` — “This command asks your DNS server to translate the domain name `vast.ai` into an IP address. The host daemon connects to Vast.ai servers by domain name, so even with a working internet connection, the machine cannot reach Vast.ai if DNS resolution is broken.” — **Responsible role:** Host implementation source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Bind an exact canonical source, authorized runtime observation, or accountable-owner decision before changing status.
- [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — Check DNS resolution](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#check-dns-resolution) — `CHC-MACHINE-OFFLINE-DNS-SCOPE-01` — “What to look for: a `Name:` line followed by an `Address:` line containing an IP address shows that the configured resolver answered this query (the exact IP returned may differ). `;; connection timed out; no servers could be reached` or `server can't find vast.ai: NXDOMAIN` shows that this query did not resolve; it does not by itself identify a global DNS fault. Involve the network owner before changing resolver or network configuration.” — **Responsible role:** Network or API source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Obtain network-owner confirmation before prescribing resolver changes.
- [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — Check your public IPv4 address](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#check-your-public-ipv4-address) — `CHC-MACHINE-OFFLINE-WEB-REQUEST-SCOPE-01` — “This command contacts an external service (`ifconfig.me`) and returns the public IPv4 address it reports for this request. - [Offline Machine - Troubleshooting Steps — 2. Confirm the Vastai Service Is Running](http://127.0.0.1:4000/host/machine-offline#2-confirm-the-vastai-service-is-running) — `CUR-c0bec3766bdb0a10` — “- **A very recent start time is a warning sign.** If you run `systemctl status vastai` repeatedly and the "ago" value keeps resetting to a few seconds or minutes (and the `Main PID` keeps changing), the service is crashing and being restarted over and over — a **restart loop**. The state `activating (auto-restart)` with a message like `Restart=on-failure` or `Scheduled restart job` also indicates a restart loop. In that case, inspect the logs (see the debugging commands below) to find the crash reason.” — **Responsible role:** Host implementation source owner. **Required:** CANONICAL_IMPLEMENTATION_SOURCE, RUNTIME_OR_UI_OBSERVATION. **Next:** Bind an exact canonical source, authorized runtime observation, or accountable-owner decision before changing status.
